<h1>Online Head Shop</h1>
<p class="h5" style="line-height: 36px;">Smoke Cartel is an online headshop dedicated to delivering premium <a href="/collections/bongs-and-water-pipes">glass bongs</a>, <a href="/collections/dab-rigs-and-oil-rigs">dab rigs</a> and <a href="/collections/glass-hand-pipes">hand pipes</a> of all stripes, <a href="/collections/vaporizers">vaporizers</a>, <a href="/collections/dab-pens-wax-vaporizers">dab pens</a> and other cool counterculture tools. From hemp goods to <a href="/collections/grinders">herb grinders</a>, travel pouches to butane torch lighters, we have the gear to make the perfect heady experience!</p>
<p class="h5" style="line-height: 36px;"><a href="https://www.globenewswire.com/news-release/2018/06/08/1519194/0/en/SMOKE-CARTEL-WINS-AWARD-FOR-GLASS-PRODUCTS-AT-HIGH-TIMES-CANNABIS-CUP.html">Winner of the High Times Cannabis Cup Best Glass Award</a> and featured on Forbes, Leafly and Weedmaps, Smoke Cartel has helped bring the quality accessories to thousands of satisfied customers! We are available anywhere, 24 hours a day, with the best smoking gear available.</p>

<script type="text/javascript">	
function pageInit() {
	var featured = $('#index-featured');
	var gettrending = false;
	var carthandles = '';
	
	featured.royalSlider({
    	autoScaleSlider: true,
    	autoScaleSliderWidth: 1120,
        autoScaleSliderHeight: 360,
        imgWidth: 1120,
    	keyboardNavEnabled: true,
    	arrowsNavHideOnTouch: true,
    	loop: true,
    	slidesSpacing: 0,
        imageScalePadding: 0,
        controlNavigation: 'bullets',
    	autoPlay: {
    		enabled: true,
    		pauseOnHover: true,
    		delay: 5000,
    	}
    });
	
	activateCountdownMeters();
	countDown(0, '.flashsale-countdown');
				
	setTimeout(function(){
		$(window).trigger('resize');
	}, 4000);
				
	if (!firstImpression()) {
		var sliderInstance = featured.data('royalSlider');

		var handles = '';
		if(typeof Cookies2.get('smokecartel_recently_viewed') !== 'undefined') { handles = Cookies2.get('smokecartel_recently_viewed'); }
		$.getJSON("/cart.js", function(n, e) {
			window.ShopifyCart = n;
		    n.item_count > 0 && $.each(n.items, function(n, e) {
		        -1 == handles.indexOf(e.handle) && (handles = e.handle + " " + handles), carthandles += " " + e.handle
		    });
		    
		    $.getJSON("//warely.io/apps/recommendations/?cart_token="+n.token+"&limit=20&scenario=homepage_recs&type=items-to-user&cascadeCreate=1", function(products) {	
			    if(products.length >= 10) {
					var scrollertarget = $('#recommendations-scroller');
					scrollertarget.html('');
					$.each(products, function(i, e) {
						if(carthandles.indexOf(e.handle) == -1) { 
							if(i < 3) {
								var insertindex = i;
								
if(i == 0) { insertindex += 1; }
								
								
								if(i != 0) {
									insertindex += 1;
								}
								sliderInstance.appendSlide('<div class="promobanner bannerlink smoothto" href="/products/'+e.handle+'"><a class="rsImg" href="https://warely.io/smokecartel/banners/'+e.handle+'.jpg">'+e.product_title+'</a></div>', insertindex);
							} else {
								renderScroller_new(i, e, scrollertarget); 
							}
						}
					});
					sliderInstance.goTo(0);
					sliderInstance.startAutoPlay();
					
					$('#recommendations').show();
				}
				activateSlider();
				activateVideo(ismobile);
				
			});		    
		});
		
		shuffleElements('#newandtrending-scroller');
	}
}
</script>
